justify;"><span leaf="">这几天如果刷社交平台，大概率会被陆家嘴那片大草坪刷屏。</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><section style="margin: 12px 0px 20px;text-align: center;line-height: 0;border-radius: 8px;overflow: hidden;"><section nodeleaf="" style="margin: 1.5em 8px;letter-spacing: 0.05em;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);text-align: justify;"><img data-src="https://mmbiz.qpic.cn/mmbiz_jpg/iaZPxh36Uvuz61Gf1GBRWnuaPYehg0T72ceIvnJXtnnQY88NnfBsX1ntLNrZU5HdNVibMT7zicGO2LIhnIH0bdC5g5CHl0rlADQSogJJF9RN74/640?wx_fmt=jpeg&amp;from=appmsg" class="rich_pages wxw-img" data-ratio="0.6666666666666666" data-type="jpeg" data-w="1080" height="auto" style="box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08) 0px 4px 12px;display: block;max-width: 100%;height: auto;border-radius: 8px;margin: 0px auto;" data-imgfileid="100007552" data-aistatus="1"  /></section></section></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">第十一届陆家嘴国际咖啡文化季正在中心绿地举办。</span><span style="color: rgb(17, 24, 39);font-weight: 700;"><span leaf="">五大洲 390 多个咖啡品牌</span></span><span leaf="">扎堆在草坪里，茶颜悦色的上海首秀排队甚至要一个多小时。</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">我是被朋友喊来的。他是家咖啡店主，在草坪上也摆了个摊</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">找他的路上，在一大堆手冲壶、拉花缸和浓郁的咖啡香气中间，我先撞见了一个老熟人：</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><section style="margin: 12px 0px 20px;text-align: center;line-height: 0;border-radius: 8px;overflow: hidden;"><section nodeleaf="" style="margin: 1.5em 8px;letter-spacing: 0.05em;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);text-align: justify;"><img data-src="https://mmbiz.qpic.cn/sz_mmbiz_jpg/iaZPxh36Uvuze21ynAic0TiavLwoVo8J5P2lZAy9fTbRkKnHs5EmzcTGcJ8E6aTC07ia1OlW7LyRychM21ODFgnV5sticQGHLrFAHUDic0NW5gYHU/640?wx_fmt=jpeg&amp;from=appmsg" class="rich_pages wxw-img" data-ratio="0.75" data-type="jpeg" data-w="1080" height="auto" style="box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08) 0px 4px 12px;display: block;max-width: 100%;height: auto;border-radius: 8px;margin: 0px auto;" data-imgfileid="100007555" data-aistatus="1"  /></section></section></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">今年的独家 AI 合作伙伴与官方办公支持，是&nbsp;</span><span style="color: rgb(4, 120, 87);font-weight: 700;"><span leaf="">360 纳米Work</span></span><span leaf="">。它还在草坪里搭了个显眼的展位，叫「纳米Work AI效率事务所」</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><section style="margin: 12px 0px 20px;text-align: center;line-height: 0;border-radius: 8px;overflow: hidden;"><section nodeleaf="" style="margin: 1.5em 8px;letter-spacing: 0.05em;color: rgb(63, 63, 63);text-align: justify;"><img data-src="https://mmbiz.qpic.cn/sz_mmbiz_png/iaZPxh36UvuwEabpbalCbt5nVzFW6ghE0RziciaRdqmmeHTuCQBxfiaxl7iaTsuy5L1Qj9eslFBxEWXzFOH2oEaNSdfibcMrRdTy5PCoEib0aJZKxw/640?wx_fmt=png&amp;from=appmsg" class="rich_pages wxw-img" data-ratio="0.75" data-type="png" data-w="1080" height="auto" style="box-shadow: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.08) 0px 4px 12px;display: block;max-width: 100%;height: auto;border-radius: 8px;margin: 0px auto;" data-imgfileid="100007554" data-aistatus="1"  /></section></section></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">看到它在这儿，我一点不意外。上个月在北京 798，纳米Work 刚启动“双城千店”计划，</span><span style="color: rgb(17, 24, 39);font-weight: 700;"><span leaf="">进咖啡馆、文创店“上岗”帮店主干活</span></span><span leaf="">；上海这一站，换成了咖啡。</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><p style="margin: 0px 0px 16px;font-size: 14px;line-height: 1.9;text-align: justify;"><span leaf="">我拉上朋友一起去展位转了转。扫码用纳米Work完成任务，可以领咖啡券、算力豆和各种职场人喜欢的创意周边；在「AI 诊断台」的大屏上，可以看到</span><span style="color: rgb(17, 24, 39);font-weight: bold;border-bottom: 2px solid rgb(242, 207, 91);padding-bottom: 1px;"><span leaf="">输几个数字就能算出自家小店的保本出杯量</span></span><span leaf="">。</span></p></section><section style="padding: 0px 12px;"><section style="margin: 12px 0px 20px;text-align: center;line-height:
